1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The neuroscience variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 292 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 591 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 292 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the neuroscience structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 591. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
